# Re:Zero — Japanese main light novel V20 deep reading

## 0. Governing thesis and boundary

V20 closes the Pristella battles while making recovery a problem of bodies, recognition, knowledge and chosen obligations. The coalition returns alive, but Ricardo has lost his right forearm, Julius has lost recognition by others, Crusch remains gravely affected and transformed patients have no demonstrated remedy. An actual farewell can coexist with renewed family rejection. An emergency promise can be real while its prolonged consequences exceed the conditions anyone expected. The proposed Sage journey grows from these specific unsolved burdens.

The volume gives Theresia a substantial narrated life rather than leaving her only as Wilhelm's remembered beloved or an attributed corpse opponent. Gardening, fear of unintended harm, inherited power, flower meetings, humiliation, chosen marriage, ordinary family care and a coercive protective wound condition the present farewell. Her last actual recognition is different from Wilhelm's earlier projections. That distinction also governs Subaru's interpretation of Wilhelm: a true report about mutual love does not give him the omitted family catastrophe.

Names and voices receive equally careful separation. Lye's first-person use of Rem's wishes is appropriation; Julius can retain his own memory while becoming a stranger to friends; the self-named scarf artificial spirit is not established as the tea Witch by a shared name. The visible Anastasia body, its current operator and the sleeping human principal have different knowledge, choices and costs. These distinctions matter to cooperation and consent, including Subaru's decision to conceal the stuck-control problem while accepting Emilia's new request for consultation.

This reading admits no V21 or supplementary/IF/WN/adaptation/new-import narrative. Living E021 continues with20observed Subaru deaths/21arrived segments. Theresia's retrospective life, ordinary waking, changing body control and Al's private repetition claims add no Subaru return. The explicit afterword closes Arc5 and earns a separate checkpoint after frozen/full-gated V20; it supplies neither complete patient/family recovery nor the authorized V43 terminal architecture/state/role-gap/acquisition review. Fifteen entering person-model scopes/defaults/local documentary grades and all53entering protected artifacts remain exact.

## 3. Event-state sequence, chronology and focalization

The present action remains inside E021. The opening resumes three unfinished fronts, then follows the mixed Lye encounter, the underground rear battle, Kurgan's duel and the Astrea confrontation. Theresia's life history expands the reader's access to the past before the narrative returns to her final recognition. The coalition's reunion, council and proposed journey follow. No narrated Subaru death or successor arrival interrupts that continuity. A new chapter, a life retrospective, a change of operator and another person's account of repeated deaths are not interchangeable event triggers.

The prologue's bodily ambiguities remain meaningful until resolved. Garfiel initially suspects Capella as the blood enclosure's maker; breaking it supplies an escape without supplying its controller. The severed arm at the Second front is initially unassigned; the later explicit Ricardo/right-forearm/elbow identification controls the current reconstruction. Assigning the arm early from reader familiarity would falsify the presentation's knowledge boundary.

The duel endings also have different evidential shapes. Garfiel receives Kurgan's actual praise and sees dissolution after a costly victory. Wilhelm initially projects feeling onto an unresponsive opponent. Theresia later really recognizes him, touches him and answers his love before ashes. The final responsive phase neither validates every earlier projection nor establishes ordinary sustained resurrection. Reinhard's assertion that a dead person should not move is a moral/body interpretation, not an independently tested rule excluding every brief returned response.

The reunion establishes living return by the coalition's principals, not a comprehensive census of everyone endangered. Mimi is awake but reopens her wound. Otto cannot yet walk normally; Garfiel is already treating him periodically. Ricardo's forearm remains lost and Julius's recognition by others is gone. Sirius is held locally; Guard transport is agreed but unperformed. Emilia proposes preservation of transformed people and the group plans a Sage inquiry; neither new ice treatment nor departure/arrival is performed. Puck's pendant is acquired while his emergence remains explicitly blocked by insufficient mana.

## 5. Character states, self-models and material revisions

Theresia receives the largest developmental expansion. Her early fear of wounding people is grounded in Death God from birth, and her gardening inheritance at twelve is a different event. Sword competence arrives before willingness; repeated pressure and the later named Sword God interpretation do not make her wishes identical to the role. The three brothers, unnamed uncle and concealed defeat turn personal delay into family/public burden. The narrative's loose temporal intervals should remain loose rather than being forced into invented birthdays and exact war calendars.

The flower meetings with Wilhelm introduce ordinary attachment before a simple rescue legend could absorb it. After three months Theresia initiates an exchange of names; Wilhelm changes the meaning of his sword, and she eventually chooses fighting to save him, but her superiority also humiliates him. His later trained victory in the permitted ceremonial duel fulfills a relational promise without requiring an intentionally thrown match or transferred blessing. Marriage/retirement quiet the voice without removing Sword Saint. Their domestic care includes the next generation's illness, disappointment and excessive burden.

Her protective choice is also fallible and coercive: deliberately wounding Wilhelm to keep him from following uses bodily leverage against a loved person. Loving purpose does not supply his assent. During the Whale battle the blessing leaves while she lives; her practiced technique survives. The platinum-haired visitor and lost consciousness supply a limited encounter, not a scene-explicit name or exact killing/reanimation mechanism. Her final own feeling loves the descendants whom Heinkel later claims she resents. Brief actual reunion with Wilhelm matters without becoming the entire family's repair.

Wilhelm's grief changes form rather than ending cleanly. He wants an impossible prolonged duel/reunion, initially projects emotion and loses a decisive opening when Heinkel arrives. He still tries to protect his son. After the actual farewell he gathers ashes and rejects help from Reinhard, conditioning conversation on regret. His earlier private exculpation of the child remains real, but it is not an apology the grandson receives. Subaru's later belief that the sword demon finished his own duel and found peace follows an incomplete account.

Reinhard's strength now has a concrete family and ordinary-life context. He can protect his father and act within Guard duty, yet those acts expose him to accusations and grandfather rejection. The pause/breath/downcast aftermath prevents an emotionless-weapon reading. Earlier excellent cooking and a received request for advice supply a separate noncombat route; neither the domestic smile nor the current right-action conviction closes family wounds. His two-year-prior inability to approach the tower is a reported access failure with a suspected barrier, not a revealed opponent stronger in combat.

Felt shows political disagreement, practical error and personal loyalty in the same sequence. She corrects Otto's dismissal of others' meaningful risks, first misuses the staff, accepts Beatrice's help and depends on injured Gaston's return. The attempted consumption fails, and Louis attributes that failure to a mismatch between Felt’s used and true names; her birth identity is not revealed and a universal adopted-name rule is unproved. Choosing to accompany Reinhard because he should not face pain alone does not require agreement with his prisoner policy or total obedience to him. Earlier rough/refined register, preferred clothing, quiz/holiday incentive and family-like Rom bond give that choice ordinary continuity.

Garfiel's victory supplies changed self-understanding and bodily cost together. Ground contact does not let repair outrun every injury. The painless corpse's missed wrist damage contributes without becoming the sole explanation of the win. Garfiel's remembered attachments become reasons to grow; the children's aid is received, while their relationship remains hidden from them. Actual later care for awake Mimi and injured Otto extends useful responsibility without erasing the broken retreat or supplying unspoken forgiveness.

Julius loses social recognition while retaining the person who remembers his own commitments. The earlier uneasy brother gap remains when Subaru identifies comatose Joshua: an explanation is not restored familiarity. Ricardo's right-forearm loss and forgetting make protection more painful than a neat successful assignment. Subaru rejects a hierarchy in which Julius's suffering must be smaller because someone else has suffered more; renewed banter helps him act, not recover his name. Their prior friendship and the new secret kept from Julius must both remain in the current model.

Beatrice's return is a completed change with a finite cause. Her available mana comes from an inefficient seven-crystal workaround; specific counters spend it. She chooses to act for a mixed group, is enraged by Lye's appropriation of Rem and later accepts the reunion hug/defends Subaru. Broader attachment does not erase old grief or pressure, convert every choice into obedience, restore the library reserve or repair Subaru's Gate. Al's help adds a particular debt of gratitude without removing her nickname boundary or the party's questions.

The apparent Anastasia and the scarf spirit require different predicates. Subaru's detection rests partly on his expectation that human Anastasia would react more personally to lasting retainer injury; this is his grounded character judgment, not a new awake Anastasia interior. The operator openly values Anastasia as family, with gestures supporting affection, but professes indifference to the other victims as such and uses common goals instrumentally. A shared name triggers Subaru's fear of the tea Witch; the disclosure corrects that easy equation rather than proving a decade of Witch freedom or a complete city plot.

Al's useful actions coexist with deeply limited disclosure. The underground exchange offers accusations, responsive disguise, demonstrated tactics and private repetition terminology. These are more evidence than the earlier unfinished confrontation, while still withholding a complete ability rule or confession to civic killings/gate opening. Sirius's local custody similarly changes her practical status without turning restraint into safety, her Geuse insistence into fact or her distorted musical lesson into benevolent growth. Capella's regeneration and coercion remain demonstrated without royal pedigree, limitless immortality or a working cure.

## 6. Relationship, disclosure, obligation and consent

The volume repeatedly separates a true relation from an imagined receipt. Garfiel can accept the children as siblings without telling them. Julius can remember a friendship whose other party no longer recognizes him. Wilhelm can truthfully report mutual love while withholding the conversation that would change Subaru's conclusion. The scarf spirit can have a real emergency promise with Anastasia while neither providing a new awake answer nor explaining why the temporary arrangement cannot end. These are different asymmetries, not one generic secrecy defect.

The final Astrea scenes oppose three demands that cannot be made equivalent. Theresia wants the descendants' happiness and gives Wilhelm a genuine farewell. Heinkel interprets her through grievance and threatens his son. Wilhelm wants regret from Reinhard, who maintains that the intervention was right while showing pain. No source-grounded analysis can turn that sequence into a completed apology, declare every loving impulse harmless or treat the grandson's moral claim as evidence he feels nothing.

Subaru receives much genuine help, yet keeps a private willingness to spend his life if an ally fails to return. The successful return relieves that immediate pressure without proving the disposition cured. His later concealment of the stuck operator problem aims to prevent opposition to the journey. Emilia's consultation request is a new prospective term that makes this an ongoing information problem; it is not the old origin conversation or until-morning apology performed, nor proof that every unshown future decision has already breached the new promise.

Mimi's waking changes what can now be selected for her voice and choices. It does not retroactively give consent to intensified sharing, the earlier concealed assault or Garfiel's broken retreat. Their present affection/care remains actual without an explicit forgiveness scene or a settled reciprocal couple. Crusch's earlier refusal likewise remains hers while she is offstage. Subaru's last-resort offer and Ferris's protective wishes are separate speakers' choices, not a new patient authorization.

The former wives' mourning supplies continuing lives after rescue rather than a completed administrative settlement. Sylphy's self-name, refusal, changed cooperation and solidarity survive the collective53-person outcome. Each woman still has a different unenumerated future; rescue gratitude is not endless sacrifice debt. Liliana's waking performance also changes the earlier endpoint without making her private imagined affiliation with Kiritaka a spoken romantic contract or pardoning his old assault.

All155entering relationship rows have individual material decisions:65advance through explicitly authored current-cell updates and90remain literal at their selected earlier phases. Sixteen new R156–171 distinguish the new family, operator, aid, knowledge and candidate-rival relations;43new RT364–406 route all changed/new cases. All363older transitions and complete affected entering rows/prior ancestry remain recoverable. These figures identify maintenance coverage; the substantive judgments are the named conditions above, not a numerical readiness threshold.


## 7. Institutions, factions and practical agency

The city has recovered control before it has recovered its people. Kiritaka's victory announcement and the return of the principal coalition are concrete institutional results. The next discussion immediately exposes insufficient healers, scarce expertise, unrecovered bodies/identities and a need for continued defense. Garfiel's periodic care and reconstruction are already occurring; recruitment from neighboring cities is reported as an effort, not a completed supply of clinicians. Otto's remaining local errand is explicit rather than completed by intention.

The mixed Lye resistance distributes agency among a candidate, an officer, retainers, company soldiers and an artificial spirit. Felt's correction of Otto matters because useful manpower is not exhausted by an emotional-fever explanation. Gaston's training and injured return also matter beside the beam. At the same time, dead recruited dragons, injured defenders and stolen recognition prevent a costless-cooperation account. Uniforms remain when names cease to be recognized; institutional traces, bodies and personal memory cannot be treated as one automatically erased object.

Felt's decision separates political agreement from loyalty. Reinhard's proposed Guard escort is an institutional responsibility; she disagrees yet chooses to accompany a distressed person. Heinkel's failed guard and subsequent accusations change practical family pressure without performing an estate succession, eviction or adjudicated kidnapping/murder. Neither the strongest family member nor an act of rescue automatically supplies every legal entitlement.

The transformed patients produce a real policy disagreement. Ferris distinguishes species reconstruction from injury or illness he can heal. Priscilla's judgment that such people would want death is her judgment; Emilia's ice-sleep proposal buys hoped-for time, and Liliana's life pledge is sincere but methodless. Council assent does not show a new procedure performed or every patient's informed will received. These choices should remain ethically legible without the analyst inventing patient testimony.

The current Anastasia body can participate in civic/travel decisions while its human principal sleeps. The emergency agreement has positive narrative support; it is not appropriate to erase it merely because the spirit is interested. Equally, the unprecedented inability to return creates a different current problem. Body control does not prove a permanent transfer of candidacy/company office or an awake human agreement to every subsequent risk. The coalition's provisional common goal is compatible with the spirit's narrower motive.

Historical institutions complicate simple heroic biography. Kurgan's service describes one status route under Imperial martial merit, not a realm without prejudice. Theresia's kingdom protects a morale symbol by concealing defeat, while her family lives the private losses. The royal-permitted duel and chosen marriage are specific events, not a universal equation among force, blessing, headship and consent. Wilhelm's royal-child search identifies an official burden and an unnamed girl; it does not decide old criminal allegations or Felt's lineage.

## 8. Mechanics, bodily costs and rival models

| question / model | observed or source-supported advance | continuing limit |
|---|---|---|
| Beatrice refill | Actual waking through inefficient forced crystal extraction; seven starting crystals and five spent before later healing | Ordinary special contract remains; no restored library reserve or arbitrary normal donor |
| Familiar offensive tools | Staff misuse is corrected through joint help; Otto's prepared stones and Gaston's learned Flow have practical effects | No measured Reinhard kill, unlimited spontaneous explosive power or named gardener |
| Gluttony trigger | Named touch/palm-lick sequences and a failed Felt attempt permit comparison | Used/true-name explanation attributed; exact global content/range/record law remains untested |
| Roy and Louis | Appropriated techniques and a third self-named changed presentation are directly encountered | No full ingestion/shared-body ontology, same-person-by-office or complete roster |
| Garfiel | Ground-linked staged repair, local floor work, severe injuries and concentration permit a costly win | Repair can be outpaced; subjective slowness is not external time stop; no imported Frederica full-feline speech/healing |
| Kurgan / Theresia | Martial selectivity/final praise and brief actual reciprocal recognition precede separate dissolutions | No sustained living resurrection, complete retained soul or present operator identified |
| Distinct blessings | Death God birth/later control differs from Sword Saint inheritance/retirement/living succession | No child-chosen theft, universal divine intention or all-blessing procedure |
| Distinct swords | Reid is drawn in the present; ceremonial duel sword and Heinkel's Astrea gift retain different identities | Tradition is not an exhaustive draw/target law |
| Capella | Speaking severed-head regrowth, discarded residue and rat reconstitution strengthen observed regeneration | No completed final death/return, infinite reserve, original body or Divine Dragon identity |
| Al | Actual earth attacks/collapse escape coexist with private approximate repetition/domain claims | No fully displayed iteration sequence or new Subaru return |
| Blood / healing | Al's condition-limited account and Capella's death-cure denial add testimony; clinical limits remain real | No provenance/compatibility equation, safe universal uptake or independently proved permanent cure impossibility |
| Bodily alteration | New species differ from injury; unrecovered Ricardo forearm cannot presently be regrown | No performed new ice preservation or all future powers prohibited by current inability |
| Gate interfaces | Anastasia import, Reinhard export and Subaru break/drain cases are separately described | Not one generic defect, all-purpose antimagic or repaired Gate |
| Od borrowing | Finite internal mana/lifespan cost, supported emergency promise and stuck fifth return are described | No exact cause, current awake indefinite assent or continuous decade of possession |
| Recovery plans | Gifted pendant and proposed Sage inquiry have identifiable stages | Puck still absent, route/cure unverified, transport/departure unperformed |
| Greed factor | Subaru privately identifies Sloth/Greed after the earlier ingress | No named/performed Greed effect, acquired Lust or inherited whole Regulus/automatic Lion Heart |

The useful comparison is among interfaces and tests rather than a generic category of powerful magic. Reinhard's inability to cast ordinarily does not contradict ambient-spirit help. Beatrice's forced use of crystals does not silently rewrite her normal contract. The scarf spirit's inability to contract and Anastasia's deficient intake are different parts of their account. Biological-looking recovery, a new species, coma, lost recognition and a temporarily controlled body likewise require separate models.

Capella's death-cure denial and Subaru's proposed factor remedy remain competing interested or speculative accounts. The volume performs neither a terminal killing/clearance experiment nor Lust acquisition. Al's experience cannot make Crusch's different symptoms safe, and Subaru's retained functional leg does not supply the unseen regrowth actor or a universal all-wound cure. Expert limits should be kept without declaring every imaginable future remedy impossible.

The defeated corpse bodies resist another shortcut. Painlessness matters to Kurgan's missed wound, while the text refuses to make that wound the sole reason Garfiel wins. Theresia has a real last responsive phase after earlier apparent emotion is explicitly projected. Neither case licenses a binary in which every corpse action is either a freely restored full person or completely empty motion. The source leaves the complete controller/soul mechanism unresolved.

## 10. Japanese wording, attribution and recipient effects

Japanese wording changes the analysis at the level of speaker and evidential status. Lye's borrowed Rem first person must retain its hostile current speaker. A familiar sentence or internal wish does not place the original person awake in the scene. Louis's self-name and the changed inner presentation establish more than an ordinary disguise, while leaving the complete body/identity rule open.

The side of the hand matters in the failed attack: the right-hand grasp and the other, left palm's lick must not be swapped. The real-name explanation is a statement by Louis, not an analyst-supplied birth name. The surviving uniforms and forgotten personal recognition are separate referents. Roy's 月食/絶掌 wording and voices add performed appropriation without converting an interested tally into inspected history.

Theresia's 幻聴 and the later Sword God label belong to subjective experience. Wilhelm's perceived deity laughter also requires its focal owner. Conversely Kurgan's 見事 is an actual final word, and Theresia's final reciprocal love is actually addressed to Wilhelm. Her unasked first-sight question and his unfinished private if-you-were-my phrase have no completed recipient receipt. Quotation marks alone cannot erase the difference between memory, thought, projection, actual speech and unspoken desire.

The sword names retain three objects: drawn Reid, the remembered ceremonial blade and Astrea given to Heinkel. Likewise Death God, Sword Saint, the retained trained skill and the family interpretations of succession are not a single noun for power or guilt. Luanna's named illness and the unnamed kidnapped daughter do not inherit Gluttony/Felt identities from reader familiarity.

The scarf encounter makes register a discriminating cue. The occupied body's familiar voice and attempted うち manner are separated from the operator's ボク register and roundabout phrasing. Subaru's coined 襟ドナ names the encounter in relation to the scarf; it does not solve creator/tea/corpse/Omega identity. The spirit's アナ affection and the described オド procedure require their own source plane, including the narrative support for an emergency promise and the missing new awake answer.

Sirius's 怨樂 is a hostile appropriation of the musical lesson, not proof she has gained a new measured power or become benevolent. Her Geuse insistence remains her proposition; narrative delusion language supplies counterweight without needing a clinical diagnosis. The Gospel convergence claim and lack of a useful central location remain attributed information, not omniscient organizational knowledge.

Tentative evidential language also matters. Priscilla calls the Kiritaka-survival explanation inference; Capella suspects Al and receives evasion; Reinhard proposes a barrier explanation for failed access; Subaru draws a conditional creator/tower connection. These are usable hypotheses with different witnesses, rather than independently completed proofs. The actual final consultation request is a prospective term, distinct from an earlier promise's unseen fulfillment.

Remaining source questions include the blood enclosure's maker, exact Gluttony content/agent for Joshua and the unknown sleepers, Felt's other name, complete Lye/Louis body relation, present corpse controller, identities/acts at Theresia's last encounter, the stuck-return cause and embedded route's origin, dragon-blood compatibility/cure, bones engineering and the alleged civic culprit. None is filled by fame, a shared name, future lore or a passing structural check. Ram's nearly-seven/nine-year chronology, the old specific apology/origin exchange, missing family disclosures and unperformed treatments likewise remain bounded gaps.

The native receipts protect original witness identity, all53entering frozen/model artifacts, prior claim/relationship ancestry, the complete incoming seal and unaffected bytes. They establish what survived a transformation and where text exists. The substantive claims still depend on actual reading, named attribution, material counterevidence and the completed saved-content/name-reference review.

## 15. Qualitative readiness and architecture responsibility

Master section13 owns this current decision; the frozen account records what was earned at the completed V20 boundary.

The source-complete V20 operation actually reaches the narrative and package EOF after the separate Liliana19/Regulus19 full gates closed. All393predecessors have individual208PRESERVE/141STRENGTHEN/36REVISE/8OPEN decisions and30new propositions make423current claims. All60incoming questions/10forecasts have separate answers. The155relationship decisions produce65explicit current updates/90literal preserves plus16new duties/43transitions. These counts identify evidence and maintenance; the readiness judgments below follow the changed conditions.

#### Arc5 checkpoint — earned, separate construction after full V20 gate

The final contributing volume's fronts settle and its actual afterword explicitly closes Arc5. **ARC_05_CHECKPOINT is EARNED_UNIMPLEMENTED.** It must be constructed as a separate admitted-source synthesis after V20's frozen reading, saved-content/name-reference acceptance and complete publication/integration gate. Its own gate precedes the newly earned person constructions below and any V21 opening. The checkpoint should join interdependent fronts, incomplete recovery, bodies/names/recipient knowledge, heroic self-spending, family rejection, coercive love and provisional institutional choices. Arc closure does not require pretending patients are cured or every mystery is solved.

The checkpoint's admitted primary span is the completed Arc5 main-LN V16–V20, with exact earlier causal/character routes retained where necessary. It opens no supplementary/adaptation/IF/WN or later narrative. It creates no final-series, independent runtime or global capability grade.

#### Theresia — MONOGRAPH_READY, construction and separate review earned

V20 changes the available question from reconstructing a husband's remembered beloved/current opponent to reconstructing a source-bounded person across consequential development. Birth-given wounding danger, gardening inheritance at12, sword aversion, three differently placed brothers/uncle losses and public symbolic pressure condition her early choices. Repeated yellow-flower meetings, pride/conflict with Wilhelm, chosen fighting, trained ceremonial defeat, marriage and ordinary family care supply noncombat and relational conditions. Deliberately wounding Wilhelm to protect him supplies a material counterexample to costless/fully recipient-respecting care. Blessing loss leaves practiced skill; the last recognition and words differ from earlier projected emotion. No complete deity/visitor/controller theory is needed to model these supported distinctions.

Earned operation: **THERESIA_V01_V20_MODEL_AND_FIDELITY_REVIEW**, after the full Arc5 checkpoint gate. Construct the integrated conditional model and a separate actually applied existing19-dimension review, with explicit confidence/rationale, documentary counterexamples and NONCANONICAL probes. They are currently **UNIMPLEMENTED/UNGRADED**, not a passed model inferred from this readiness decision.

Proposed source-bounded default: **V20_E021_TERMINAL_ASHES_NO_CURRENT_LIVING_REPLY**. An earlier living, retrospective or brief final-responsive use needs explicit development/event/knowledge/remembered-history/recipient/role-status/body-resource coordinates. Do not produce a living present answer, unheard first-sight question, every descendant's received apology, a named fog visitor or psychiatric/divine diagnosis. Master owns readiness; future local model/review own only their declared completed scope after acceptance.

#### Reinhard — MONOGRAPH_READY, construction and separate review earned

The new source adds a discriminating self/relationship condition to established breadth. Earlier rescues, public courtesy, candidate service, coercive family pressure and qualified independent action now meet direct protection of the father, the grandmother's destruction/brief farewell, painful rejection and a paused right-action/no-regret answer. These are materially different from simply winning a battle. The source shows distress rather than an affectless weapon. Earlier excellent cooking, Felt's appreciation and Wilhelm's received request for advice supply a domestic competence route; friendship/help with Subaru and respectful cooperation with peers supply different recipients. The reported failed tower approach supplies an access limit without needing a stronger opponent or full blessing inventory.

Earned operation: **REINHARD_V01_V20_MODEL_AND_FIDELITY_REVIEW**, after Theresia's own complete gate. Apply the same integrated seven-axis construction and separate19-dimension review rather than extending any other character's grade. It is **UNIMPLEMENTED/UNGRADED**.

Proposed default: **V20_E021_POSTBATTLE_GUARD_DUTY_BEFORE_UNPERFORMED_SIRIUS_ESCORT**. Preserve hurt, current family rupture, voluntary candidate relationship and task constraints. No discarded E020 memory, received old grandfather apology, universal blessing/healing/revival, regained Julius familiarity or completed escort is implied. Earlier ordinary/family phases require a supported override; unknown powers and childhood causation require abstention rather than being made promotion blockers or invented answers.

#### Felt — MONOGRAPH_READY, construction and separate review earned

Felt's source pattern now supports a bounded whole-person question across learned performance, attachment, command, risk and correction. The earlier Rom/loot-house relation and rejection/conditional acceptance of imposed noble role join preferred familiar clothes, performed refined greeting and a book-quiz/holiday incentive. The current volume adds a reasoned defense of meaningful voluntary risk, an actual tool mistake/received correction, dependence on Gaston's injured choice and a failed name attack whose explanation does not supply her birth identity. Her decision to care for Reinhard while opposing his policy shows independent political judgment and chosen loyalty in the same event. This is more than a repeated defiant mannerism or an invariant subordinate role.

Earned operation: **FELT_V01_V20_MODEL_AND_FIDELITY_REVIEW**, after Reinhard's own complete gate. Use explicit recipient, ordinary, competence/failure, voice/register, negative-control and abstention conditions and apply the separate existing19-dimension review. It is **UNIMPLEMENTED/UNGRADED**.

Proposed default: **V20_E021_POSTRESISTANCE_CANDIDATE_BEFORE_PROPOSED_ESCORT_DEPARTURE**. No unknown birth name, proved royal lineage, estate transfer, universal alias immunity or total obedience is available. Rom has no invented current reply. Earlier hostile/etiquette/household phases retain different knowledge and bodily conditions.
